111: function getHiddenColumns() {
112:   try {
113:     const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PAGE5_HIDDEN_COLUMNS_KEY) || "{}");
114:     if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
115:       return parsed.reduce((acc, key) => {
116:         acc[key] = true;
117:         return acc;
118:       }, {});
119:     }
120:     return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : {};
121:   } catch (e) {
122:     return {};
123:   }
124: }

126: function saveHiddenColumns() {
127:   localStorage.setItem(PAGE5_HIDDEN_COLUMNS_KEY, JSON.stringify(hiddenColumns || {}));
128: }
129: 
130: function isColumnHidden(key) {
131:   return activeTab === "jc" && Boolean(hiddenColumns?.[key]);
132: }
133: 
134: function getVisibleColumns(cols) {
135:   if (activeTab !== "jc") return cols || [];
136:   const baseCols = (isDispatchUser() ? DISPATCH_COLS : (cols || []))
137:     .filter(c => c.key !== "is_priority" || window._canSeePriorityColumn !== false);
138:   const visible = baseCols.filter(c => !isColumnHidden(c.key));
139:   return visible.length ? visible : baseCols;
140: }
141: 
142: function hideColumn(key) {
143:   if (!key) return false;
144:   const visibleCount = getVisibleColumns(TAB.jc?.cols || []).length;
145:   if (visibleCount <= 1 && !hiddenColumns[key]) {
146:     showToast("At least one PPC column must remain visible", "error");
147:     return false;
148:   }
149:   hiddenColumns[key] = true;
150:   saveHiddenColumns();
151:   return true;
152: }
153: 
154: function showAllColumns() {
155:   const menu = document.getElementById("column-chooser-menu");
156:   if (!menu) return;
157:   menu.querySelectorAll("input[type='checkbox']").forEach(ch => {
158:     ch.checked = true;
159:   });
160:   // Apply immediately
161:   hiddenColumns = {};
162:   saveHiddenColumns();
163:   closeColumnChooser();
164:   loadData();
165: }

201: function renderColumnChooser() {
202:   const menu = document.getElementById("column-chooser-menu");
203:   if (!menu) return;
204: 
205:   const baseCols = (isDispatchUser() ? DISPATCH_COLS : TAB.jc.cols)
206:     .filter(c => c.key !== "is_priority" || window._canSeePriorityColumn !== false);
207: 
208:   menu.innerHTML = `
209:     <div class="column-chooser-title">Columns</div>
210:     <div class="column-chooser-list">
211:       ${baseCols.map(c => `
212:         <label class="column-chooser-row">
213:           <input
214:             type="checkbox"
215:             value="${escapeHtml(c.key)}"
216:             ${hiddenColumns[c.key] ? "" : "checked"}
217:           />
218:           <span>${escapeHtml(c.label)}</span>
219:         </label>
220:       `).join("")}
221:     </div>
222:     <div class="column-chooser-actions">
223:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-show-all" onclick="showAllColumns()">Show All</button>
224:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-cancel" onclick="closeColumnChooser()">Cancel</button>
225:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-apply" onclick="applyColumnChooser()">Apply</button>
226:     </div>
227:   `;
228: }

219:         </label>

224:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-cancel" onclick="closeColumnChooser()">Cancel</button>
225:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-apply" onclick="applyColumnChooser()">Apply</button>
226:     </div>

229: function applyColumnChooser() {
230:   const menu = document.getElementById("column-chooser-menu");
231:   if (!menu) return;
232: 
233:   const nextHidden = {};
234:   let checkedCount = 0;
235:   menu.querySelectorAll("input[type='checkbox']").forEach(ch => {
236:     if (ch.checked) checkedCount++;
237:     else nextHidden[ch.value] = true;
238:   });
239: 
240:   if (!checkedCount) {
241:     showToast("At least one PPC column must remain visible", "error");
242:     return;
243:   }
244: 
245:   hiddenColumns = nextHidden;
246:   saveHiddenColumns();
247:   closeColumnChooser();
248:   loadData();
249: }

256:   ).join("");
257: }

221:     </div>
222:     <div class="column-chooser-actions">
223:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-show-all" onclick="showAllColumns()">Show All</button>
224:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-cancel" onclick="closeColumnChooser()">Cancel</button>
225:       <button type="button" class="column-chooser-apply" onclick="applyColumnChooser()">Apply</button>
226:     </div>
227:   `;
228: }
229: function applyColumnChooser() {
230:   const menu = document.getElementById("column-chooser-menu");
231:   if (!menu) return;
232: 
233:   const nextHidden = {};
234:   let checkedCount = 0;
235:   menu.querySelectorAll("input[type='checkbox']").forEach(ch => {
236:     if (ch.checked) checkedCount++;
237:     else nextHidden[ch.value] = true;
238:   });
239: 
240:   if (!checkedCount) {
241:     showToast("At least one PPC column must remain visible", "error");
242:     return;
243:   }
244: 
245:   hiddenColumns = nextHidden;
246:   saveHiddenColumns();
247:   closeColumnChooser();
248:   loadData();
249: }
250: 
251: hiddenColumns = getHiddenColumns();

253: function renderSupervisorOptions() {
254:   return (supervisorOptions || []).map(s =>
255:     `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${String(s.id) === String(selectedSupervisorUserId) ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.username)}</option>`
256:   ).join("");
257: }
258: 
259: function getSelectedSupervisorId() {
260:   const selected = getFilterVal("supervisor_user_id");
261:   return selected || selectedSupervisorUserId;
262: }
263: 
264: function getSelectedSupervisorName() {
265:   const id = getSelectedSupervisorId();
266:   if (!id) return "";
267:   const match = (supervisorOptions || []).find(s => String(s.id) === String(id));
268:   if (match) return match.username || match.full_name || "";
269:   const el = document.querySelector(`#filter-panel-content [data-param="supervisor_user_id"]`);
270:   return el?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent?.trim() || "";
271: }

275:   if (summary) return summary;
276:

279:

282:   summary.style.display = "none";
283:   summary.style.margin = "0 0 10px";
284:   summary.style.alignItems = "center";
285:   summary.style.gap = "8px";
286:   summary.style.flexWrap = "wrap";
287:   searchRow.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", summary);
288:   return summary;
289: }

385:   const wsView = document.getElementById("ws-view");
386:   if (wsView) wsView.style.display = isWs ? "block" : "none";

366: // ── Switch tab ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
367: function switchTab(tab) {
368:   activeTab = tab;
369:   currentPage = 1;
370:   sortCol = "";
371:   sortOrder = "desc";
372:   filterOpen = false;
373:   closeColumnChooser();
374:   closeExcelFilterMenu();
375: 
376:   document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
377:   document.getElementById("tab-" + tab).classList.add("active");
378: 
379:   const isReport = false;
380:   const isPlan = false;
381:   const isWs = tab === "ws";
382:   const isAr = false;
383: 
384:   document.getElementById("table-view").style.display = (!isWs) ? "block" : "none";
385:   const wsView = document.getElementById("ws-view");
386:   if (wsView) wsView.style.display = isWs ? "block" : "none";
387: 
388:   const columnsBtn = document.getElementById("btn-columns");
389:   if (columnsBtn) columnsBtn.style.display = tab === "jc" ? "" : "none";
390: 
391:   // Hide filter panel on tab switch
392:   const panel = document.getElementById("filter-panel");
393:   panel.style.display = "none";
394:   document.getElementById("btn-filter-toggle")?.classList.remove("active");
395: 
396:   document.getElementById("global-search").value = "";
397:   const prSearch = document.getElementById("pr-search");
398:   if (prSearch) prSearch.value = "";
399: 
400:   if (isReport) {
401:     prCurrentPage = 1;
402:     loadProcessReport();
403:   } else if (isPlan) loadPlanningSheet();
404:   else if (isWs) loadWipSummary();
405:   else if (isAr) loadAssemblyReadiness();
406:   else loadData();
407: }

409: // ── Filter toggle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
410: function toggleFilters() {
411:   // PPC tab uses same filter panel as other tabs
412: 
413:   filterOpen = !filterOpen;
414:   const panel = document.getElementById("filter-panel");
415:   const btn = document.getElementById("btn-filter-toggle");
416:   panel.style.display = filterOpen ? "block" : "none";
417:   btn?.classList.toggle("active", filterOpen);
418:   if (filterOpen) buildFilterPanel();
419: }

432:       <select data-param="wip" onchange="currentPage=1;loadData();updateFilterBadge()">
433:         <option value="">All</option>
434:         ${(wipOptions || []).map(w => `<option>${w}</option>`).join("")}
435:       </select>`);

366: // ── Switch tab ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
367: function switchTab(tab) {
368:   activeTab = tab;
369:   currentPage = 1;
370:   sortCol = "";
371:   sortOrder = "desc";
372:   filterOpen = false;
373:   closeColumnChooser();
374:   closeExcelFilterMenu();
375: 
376:   document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
377:   document.getElementById("tab-" + tab).classList.add("active");
378: 
379:   const isReport = false;
380:   const isPlan = false;
381:   const isWs = tab === "ws";
382:   const isAr = false;
383: 
384:   document.getElementById("table-view").style.display = (!isWs) ? "block" : "none";
385:   const wsView = document.getElementById("ws-view");
386:   if (wsView) wsView.style.display = isWs ? "block" : "none";
387: 
388:   const columnsBtn = document.getElementById("btn-columns");
389:   if (columnsBtn) columnsBtn.style.display = tab === "jc" ? "" : "none";
390: 
391:   // Hide filter panel on tab switch
392:   const panel = document.getElementById("filter-panel");
393:   panel.style.display = "none";
394:   document.getElementById("btn-filter-toggle")?.classList.remove("active");
395: 
396:   document.getElementById("global-search").value = "";
397:   const prSearch = document.getElementById("pr-search");
398:   if (prSearch) prSearch.value = "";
399: 
400:   if (isReport) {
401:     prCurrentPage = 1;
402:     loadProcessReport();
403:   } else if (isPlan) loadPlanningSheet();
404:   else if (isWs) loadWipSummary();
405:   else if (isAr) loadAssemblyReadiness();
406:   else loadData();
407: }
408: 
409: // ── Filter toggle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
410: function toggleFilters() {
411:   // PPC tab uses same filter panel as other tabs
412: 
413:   filterOpen = !filterOpen;
414:   const panel = document.getElementById("filter-panel");
415:   const btn = document.getElementById("btn-filter-toggle");
416:   panel.style.display = filterOpen ? "block" : "none";
417:   btn?.classList.toggle("active", filterOpen);
418:   if (filterOpen) buildFilterPanel();
419: }
420:

430:       </select>`);

434:         ${(wipOptions || []).map(w => `<option>${w}</option>`).join("")}
435:       </select>`);

437:       <select data-param="status" onchange="currentPage=1;loadData();updateFilterBadge()">
438:         <option value="">All</option><option>Pending</option><option>Completed</option>

364: };

460:         <option value="yes">Urgent Only</option>
461:       </select>`);
462:     html += filterGroup("Sort By", `
463:       <select data-param="sort" onchange="debounceSearch();updateFilterBadge()">
464:         <option value="qc.checked_at">Check Date</option>
465:         <option value="qcd.item_name">Item Name</option>
466:       </select>
467:       <select data-param="order" onchange="debounceSearch();updateFilterBadge()" style="margin-top:4px;">
468:         <option value="desc">Newest First</option>
469:         <option value="asc">Oldest First</option>
470:       </select>`);
471:   }
472: 
473:   else if (activeTab === "pr") {
474:     html += filterGroup("WIP Status", `
475:       <select data-param="wip" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()">
476:         <option value="">All</option>
477:         ${(prWipOptions || []).map(w => `<option>${w}</option>`).join("")}
478:       </select>`);
479:     html += filterGroup("Process Status", `
480:       <select data-param="proc_status" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()">
481:         <option value="">All</option>
482:         <option value="Completed">Completed</option>
483:         <option value="In Progress">In Progress</option>
484:         <option value="Subcontracting">Subcontracting</option>
485:         <option value="Pending">Pending</option>
486:       </select>`);
487:     html += filterGroup("Delivery From", `<input type="date" data-param="delivery_from" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()" />`);
488:     html += filterGroup("Delivery To", `<input type="date" data-param="delivery_to"   onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()" />`);
489:     html += filterGroup("Overdue Only", `
490:       <select data-param="overdue" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()">
491:         <option value="">All</option>
492:         <option value="yes">Overdue Only</option>
493:         <option value="critical">Critical (>7d overdue)</option>
494:       </select>`);
495:     html += filterGroup("Urgent Only", `
496:       <select data-param="urgent_only" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()">
497:         <option value="">All</option>
498:         <option value="yes">Urgent Only</option>
499:       </select>`);
500:   }
501: 
502:   else if (activeTab === "ps") {
503:     html += filterGroup("WIP Status", `
504:       <select data-param="wip" onchange="loadProcessReport();updateFilterBadge()">
505:         <option value="">All</option>
506:         ${(prWipOptions || []).map(w => `<option>${w}</option>`).join("")}
507:       </select>`);
508:     html += filterGroup("Delivery From", `<input type="date" data-param="delivery_from" onchange="loadPlanningSheet();updateFilterBadge()" />`);
509:     html += filterGroup("Delivery To", `<input type="date" data-param="delivery_to"   onchange="loadPlanningSheet();updateFilterBadge()" />`);
510:     html += filterGroup("Overdue Only", `
511:       <select data-param="overdue" onchange="loadPlanningSheet();updateFilterBadge()">
512:         <option value="">All</option>
513:         <option value="yes">Overdue Only</option>
514:         <option value="critical">Critical (>7d overdue)</option>
515:       </select>`);
516:     html += filterGroup("Urgent Only", `
517:       <select data-param="urgent_only" onchange="loadPlanningSheet();updateFilterBadge()">
518:         <option value="">All</option>
519:         <option value="yes">Urgent Only</option>
520:       </select>`);
521:   }
522: 
523:   content.innerHTML = html;

530: function updateFilterBadge() {
531:   let count = 0;
532:   document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(s => { if (s.value) count++; });
533:   document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(i => { if (i.value) count++; });
534:   const badge = document.getElementById("filter-count-badge");
535:   if (badge) {
536:     badge.textContent = count;
537:     badge.style.display = count > 0 ? "inline-block" : "none";
538:   }
539: }

541: function clearAllFilters() {
542:   // PPC tab uses same filter panel as other tabs
543: 
544:   document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(s => s.value = "");
545:   document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(i => i.value = "");
546:   selectedSupervisorUserId = "";
547:   renderSupervisorFilterSummary();
548:   updateFilterBadge();
549:   if (activeTab === "qc") debounceSearch();
550:   /* removed: loadProcessReport */
551:   /* removed: loadPlanningSheet */
552: }

554: // ── Search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
555: function debounceSearch() {
556:   const val = document.getElementById("global-search").value;
557:   document.getElementById("btn-clear-search").style.display = val ? "block" : "none";
558:   clearTimeout(searchTimer);
559:   searchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
560:     currentPage = 1;
561:     if (activeTab === "pr") {
562:       prCurrentPage = 1;
563:       loadProcessReport();
564:     } /* removed: loadPlanningSheet */
565:     else loadData();
566:   }, 350);
567: }
568: 
569: function clearSearch() {
570:   document.getElementById("global-search").value = "";
571:   document.getElementById("btn-clear-search").style.display = "none";
572:   currentPage = 1;
573:   loadData();
574: }

580:     search,
581:     page: currentPage,

583:     sort: sortCol,
584:     order: sortOrder
585:   });
586:

588:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(sel => {
589:       if (sel.value) p.set(sel.dataset.param, sel.value);
590:     });
591: 
592:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(inp => {
593:       if (inp.value) p.set(inp.dataset.param, inp.value);
594:     });
595: 
596:     const supervisorSelect = document.querySelector(`#filter-panel-content [data-param="supervisor_user_id"]`);

524: }

576: // ── Build query params ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
577: function buildParams() {
578:   const search = document.getElementById("global-search").value.trim();
579:   const p = new URLSearchParams({
580:     search,
581:     page: currentPage,
582:     per_page: perPage,
583:     sort: sortCol,
584:     order: sortOrder
585:   });
586: 
587:   if (activeTab !== "jc") {
588:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(sel => {
589:       if (sel.value) p.set(sel.dataset.param, sel.value);
590:     });
591: 
592:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(inp => {
593:       if (inp.value) p.set(inp.dataset.param, inp.value);
594:     });
595: 
596:     const supervisorSelect = document.querySelector(`#filter-panel-content [data-param="supervisor_user_id"]`);
597:     if (supervisorSelect) selectedSupervisorUserId = supervisorSelect.value || "";
598:   }
599: 
600:   const filterDate = localStorage.getItem("jms_filter_date");
601:   if (filterDate) p.set("filter_date", filterDate);
602: 
603:   // Excel-like header filters for PPC tab.
604:   // Backend expects params like:
605:   // xf_wip_status=Drawing&xf_wip_status=Raw Material
606:   if (activeTab === "jc") {
607:     Object.entries(excelFilters || {}).forEach(([columnKey, selectedValues]) => {
608:       if (!Array.isArray(selectedValues) || selectedValues.length === 0) return;
609: 
610:       selectedValues.forEach(value => {
611:         p.append(`xf_${columnKey}`, value === "" ? "__BLANK__" : value);
612:       });
613:     });
614: 
615:     if (lastAuditTimeFilter.from) {

562:       prCurrentPage = 1;
563:       loadProcessReport();
564:     } /* removed: loadPlanningSheet */
565:     else loadData();
566:   }, 350);
567: }

569: function clearSearch() {
570:   document.getElementById("global-search").value = "";
571:   document.getElementById("btn-clear-search").style.display = "none";
572:   currentPage = 1;
573:   loadData();
574: }
575: 
576: // ── Build query params ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
577: function buildParams() {
578:   const search = document.getElementById("global-search").value.trim();
579:   const p = new URLSearchParams({
580:     search,
581:     page: currentPage,
582:     per_page: perPage,
583:     sort: sortCol,
584:     order: sortOrder
585:   });
586: 
587:   if (activeTab !== "jc") {
588:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(sel => {
589:       if (sel.value) p.set(sel.dataset.param, sel.value);
590:     });
591: 
592:     document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(inp => {
593:       if (inp.value) p.set(inp.dataset.param, inp.value);
594:     });
595: 
596:     const supervisorSelect = document.querySelector(`#filter-panel-content [data-param="supervisor_user_id"]`);
597:     if (supervisorSelect) selectedSupervisorUserId = supervisorSelect.value || "";
598:   }
599: 
600:   const filterDate = localStorage.getItem("jms_filter_date");
601:   if (filterDate) p.set("filter_date", filterDate);
602: 
603:   // Excel-like header filters for PPC tab.
604:   // Backend expects params like:
605:   // xf_wip_status=Drawing&xf_wip_status=Raw Material
606:   if (activeTab === "jc") {
607:     Object.entries(excelFilters || {}).forEach(([columnKey, selectedValues]) => {
608:       if (!Array.isArray(selectedValues) || selectedValues.length === 0) return;
609: 
610:       selectedValues.forEach(value => {
611:         p.append(`xf_${columnKey}`, value === "" ? "__BLANK__" : value);
612:       });
613:     });
614: 
615:     if (lastAuditTimeFilter.from) {
616:       p.set("last_audit_time_from", lastAuditTimeFilter.from);
617:     }
618: 
619:     if (lastAuditTimeFilter.to) {
620:       p.set("last_audit_time_to", lastAuditTimeFilter.to);
621:     }
622:   }
623: 
624:   return p.toString();
625: }

641: function setExcelFilterValues(columnKey, values) {
642:   const cleaned = Array.from(new Set((values || []).map(v => String(v ?? ""))));
643:   if (cleaned.length) {
644:     excelFilters[columnKey] = cleaned;
645:   } else {
646:     delete excelFilters[columnKey];
647:   }
648: }

603:   // Excel-like header filters for PPC tab.
604:   // Backend expects params like:
605:   // xf_wip_status=Drawing&xf_wip_status=Raw Material
606:   if (activeTab === "jc") {
607:     Object.entries(excelFilters || {}).forEach(([columnKey, selectedValues]) => {
608:       if (!Array.isArray(selectedValues) || selectedValues.length === 0) return;
609: 
610:       selectedValues.forEach(value => {
611:         p.append(`xf_${columnKey}`, value === "" ? "__BLANK__" : value);
612:       });
613:     });
614: 
615:     if (lastAuditTimeFilter.from) {
616:       p.set("last_audit_time_from", lastAuditTimeFilter.from);
617:     }
618: 
619:     if (lastAuditTimeFilter.to) {
620:       p.set("last_audit_time_to", lastAuditTimeFilter.to);
621:     }
622:   }
623: 
624:   return p.toString();
625: }
626: 
627: function isExcelFilterActive(columnKey) {
628:   return Array.isArray(excelFilters[columnKey]) && excelFilters[columnKey].length > 0;
629: }
630: 
631: function closeExcelFilterMenu() {
632:   const existing = document.getElementById("excel-filter-menu");
633:   if (existing) existing.remove();
634:   openExcelFilterColumn = null;
635: }
636: 
637: function getExcelFilterValues(columnKey) {
638:   return Array.isArray(excelFilters[columnKey]) ? excelFilters[columnKey] : [];
639: }
640: 
641: function setExcelFilterValues(columnKey, values) {
642:   const cleaned = Array.from(new Set((values || []).map(v => String(v ?? ""))));
643:   if (cleaned.length) {
644:     excelFilters[columnKey] = cleaned;
645:   } else {
646:     delete excelFilters[columnKey];
647:   }
648: }
649: 
650: async function fetchExcelFilterOptions(columnKey) {
651:   if (excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey]) {
652:     return excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey];
653:   }
654: 
655:   const search = document.getElementById("global-search")?.value.trim() || "";
656:   const params = new URLSearchParams({ column: columnKey });
657:   if (search) params.set("search", search);
658: 
659:   const res = await fetch(`/api/data/job_cards/filter_options?${params.toString()}`);
660:   const data = await res.json();

650: async function fetchExcelFilterOptions(columnKey) {
651:   if (excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey]) {
652:     return excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey];
653:   }
654: 
655:   const search = document.getElementById("global-search")?.value.trim() || "";
656:   const params = new URLSearchParams({ column: columnKey });
657:   if (search) params.set("search", search);
658: 
659:   const res = await fetch(`/api/data/job_cards/filter_options?${params.toString()}`);
660:   const data = await res.json();
661: 
662:   if (!data.success) {
663:     showToast(data.error || "Could not load filter values", "error");
664:     return [];
665:   }
666: 
667:   excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey] = data.values || [];
668:   return excelFilterOptionsCache[columnKey];
669: }

671: async function openExcelFilterMenu(event, columnKey, columnLabel) {
672:   event.preventDefault();
673:   event.stopPropagation();
674: 
675:   if (activeTab !== "jc") return;
676: 
677:   const button = event.currentTarget;
678: 
679:   if (openExcelFilterColumn === columnKey) {
680:     closeExcelFilterMenu();
681:     return;
682:   }
683: 
684:   closeExcelFilterMenu();
685:   openExcelFilterColumn = columnKey;
686: 
687:   const menu = document.createElement("div");
688:   menu.id = "excel-filter-menu";
689:   menu.className = "excel-filter-menu";
690:   menu.innerHTML = `
691:   <div class="excel-filter-title">${escapeHtml(columnLabel)}</div>
692: 
693:   <button type="button" class="excel-filter-sort-btn" data-sort="asc">
694:     Sort A to Z
695:   </button>
696: 
697:   <button type="button" class="excel-filter-sort-btn" data-sort="desc">
698:     Sort Z to A
699:   </button>
700: 
701:   <div class="excel-filter-separator"></div>
702: 
703:   ${columnKey === "last_audit" ? `
704:     <div class="excel-time-filter-box">
705:   <div class="excel-time-filter-title">Filter by Time</div>
706: 
707:   <div class="excel-time-filter-grid">
708:     <label class="excel-time-filter-label">
709:       From
710:       <input
711:         type="time"
712:         class="excel-last-audit-time-from"
713:         value="${escapeHtml(lastAuditTimeFilter.from || "")}"
714:       />
715:     </label>
716: 
717:     <label class="excel-time-filter-label">
718:       To
719:       <input
720:         type="time"
721:         class="excel-last-audit-time-to"
722:         value="${escapeHtml(lastAuditTimeFilter.to || "")}"
723:       />
724:     </label>
725:   </div>
726: </div>
727: 
728:     <div class="excel-filter-separator"></div>
729:   ` : ""}

936:   if (
937:     menu?.contains(event.target) ||
938:     event.target.closest(".excel-filter-btn")
939:   ) {
940:     return;
941:   }
942: 
943:   if (
944:     columnChooser?.contains(event.target) ||
945:     event.target.closest("#btn-columns")
946:   ) {
947:     return;
948:   }
949: 
950:   closeExcelFilterMenu();
951:   closeColumnChooser();
952: });

959: // ── Load data ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
960: async function loadData() {
961:   const cfg = TAB[activeTab];
962:   const visibleCols = getVisibleColumns(cfg?.cols || []);
963:   const loadingColspan = (visibleCols.length || 20) + ((activeTab === "jc" && canDeletePpcItems()) ? 1 : 0);
964:   document.getElementById("table-body").innerHTML =
965:     `<tr><td colspan="${loadingColspan}">${renderTableSkeleton(8)}</td></tr>`;
966:   try {
967:     const res = await fetch(`${cfg.api}?${buildParams()}`);
968:     const data = await res.json();
969:     if (!data.success) { showToast(data.error, "error"); return; }
970: 
971:     allData = data.data;
972:     totalRows = data.total || data.data.length;
973: 
974:     if (activeTab === "jc") {
975:       if (data.wip_options && !wipOptions.length) wipOptions = data.wip_options;
976:       statusOptions = data.status_options || statusOptions;
977:       supervisorOptions = data.supervisor_options || supervisorOptions;
978:       if (data.wo_options) woOptions = data.wo_options;
979:       if (data.can_see_rm_status !== undefined) window._canSeeRmStatus = data.can_see_rm_status;
980:       if (data.can_see_priority_column !== undefined) window._canSeePriorityColumn = data.can_see_priority_column;
981:     }
982: 
983:     const renderCols = getVisibleColumns(cfg?.cols || []);
984:     renderHead(renderCols);
985:     renderBody(renderCols, data.data);
986:     renderPagination();
987:     renderSupervisorAwareRowCount();
988:     renderSupervisorFilterSummary();
989: 
990:   } catch (e) { console.error("LOADDATA ERROR:", e); showToast("Error: " + e.message, "error"); }
991: }

1023: function getSavedColumnWidths() {
1024:   try {
1025:     return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PAGE5_COL_WIDTH_KEY) || "{}");
1026:   } catch (e) {
1027:     return {};
1028:   }
1029: }
1030: 
1031: function saveColumnWidth(tab, key, width) {
1032:   const saved = getSavedColumnWidths();
1033:   if (!saved[tab]) saved[tab] = {};
1034:   saved[tab][key] = width;
1035:   localStorage.setItem(PAGE5_COL_WIDTH_KEY, JSON.stringify(saved));
1036: }
1037: 
1038: function getColumnWidth(tab, key) {
1039:   const saved = getSavedColumnWidths();
1040:   return saved?.[tab]?.[key] || DEFAULT_COL_WIDTHS[key] || 140;
1041: }
1042: 
1043: function buildResizableColgroup(table, columns) {
1044:   const oldColgroup = table.querySelector("colgroup");
1045:   if (oldColgroup) oldColgroup.remove();
1046: 
1047:   const colgroup = document.createElement("colgroup");
1048: 
1049:   columns.forEach(col => {
1050:     const colEl = document.createElement("col");
1051:     colEl.dataset.key = col.key;
1052:     colEl.style.width = `${getColumnWidth(activeTab, col.key)}px`;
1053:     colgroup.appendChild(colEl);
1054:   });
1055: 
1056:   table.insertBefore(colgroup, table.firstChild);
1057: }
1058: 
1059: function initColumnResize(table, columns) {
1060:   const handles = table.querySelectorAll(".col-resize-handle");
1061:   const colgroup = table.querySelector("colgroup");
1062:   if (!colgroup) return;
1063: 
1064:   handles.forEach(handle => {
1065:     handle.addEventListener("click", e => e.stopPropagation());
1066: 
1067:     handle.addEventListener("mousedown", function (e) {

1195: function getWipBadgeClass(row, value) {
1196:   if (isSubcontracted(row)) return "badge-subcontract";
1197:   const s = String(value || "").toLowerCase();
1198:   if (s === "store" || s === "complete" || s === "completed") return "badge-ok";

1207: function getProcessVisualState(process, index, currentIndex, wipStatus) {
1208:   const wip = String(wipStatus || "").trim().toLowerCase();
1209:   if (wip === "store" || wip === "complete" || wip === "completed") return "completed";
1210:   if (currentIndex === -1) return process.is_completed ? "completed" : "pending";
1211:   if (index < currentIndex) return "completed";
1212:   if (index > currentIndex) return "pending";
1213:   return isSubcontracted(process) ? "subcontract" : "current";
1214: }

1216: // ── Render body ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1217: function renderBody(cols, rows) {
1218:   const tbody = document.getElementById("table-body");
1219:   const showDeleteAction = activeTab === "jc" && canDeletePpcItems();
1220:   if (!rows.length) {
1221:     tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${cols.length + (showDeleteAction ? 1 : 0)}"><div class="data-state">No records found</div></td></tr>`;
1222:     return;
1223:   }
1224:   tbody.innerHTML = rows.map((row, index) => {
1225:     const cells = cols.map(c => {
1226:       const rawValue = row[c.key];
1227:       let v = c.key === "wip_stage_days" ? (rawValue ?? 0) : formatCellValue(c.key, rawValue);
1228:       if (c.key === "job_card_no" && v) {
1229:         v = `<a class="jc-link" onclick="goToPage3('${String(v).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">${v}</a>`;
1230:         if (typeof canEditRowFields === "function" && canEditRowFields(row)) {
1231:           v += ` <i class="fa fa-pencil" style="margin-left:6px;color:var(--accent);cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;" title="Edit fields" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); openEditJobCardModalById('${String(row.job_card_no).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${String(row.item_name).replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}')"></i>`;
1232:         }
1233:       }
1234:       if (c.key === "is_priority") {
1235:         const canEditPriority = hasPage5Field(row, "is_priority");
1236:         v = `<input type="checkbox" ${row.is_priority ? "checked" : ""} ${canEditPriority ? "" : "disabled"} onchange="togglePriorityImmediate(${Number(row.item_id) || 0}, '${row.job_card_no}', '${encodeURIComponent(row.item_name)}', this.checked, this)" style="width:16px;height:16px;cursor:${canEditPriority ? "pointer" : "not-allowed"};accent-color:#dc2626;" />`;
1237:       }

1160:       class="col-resize-handle"
1161:       data-col-index="${index}"
1162:       data-col-key="${c.key}"
1163:       title="Drag to resize column"
1164:     ></span>
1165:   </th>
1166: `;
1167:   }).join("");
1168: 
1169:   const actionTh = showActionCol
1170:     ? `
1171:       <th class="resizable-th" style="text-align:center;">
1172:         <span class="th-label">Actions</span>
1173:         <span
1174:           class="col-resize-handle"
1175:           data-col-index="${cols.length}"
1176:           data-col-key="__actions"
1177:           title="Drag to resize column"
1178:         ></span>
1179:       </th>
1180:     `
1181:     : "";
1182:

1186:     initColumnResize(table, resizeCols);
1187:   }

1235:         const canEditPriority = hasPage5Field(row, "is_priority");

1240:           : v === "NOT OK" ? `<span class="badge-notok">Not OK</span>` : v;
1241:       }

1243:         v = `<span class="badge-overdue">${v}d overdue</span>`;
1244:       }

1246:         const cls = getWipBadgeClass(row, v);
1247:         const vendor = isSubcontracted(row) && row.vendor_name ? ` title="Subcontracting: ${row.vendor_name}"` : "";
1248:         const isStoreOrPending = ["store", "pending"].includes(String(rawValue || "").trim().toLowerCase());
1249:         const clickAttr = (isStoreOrPending || !canEditRowProcess(row)) ? "" : ` style="cursor:pointer;" onclick='openSharedStageModal(${JSON.stringify(row.job_card_no)}, ${JSON.stringify(row.item_name)})'`;
1250:         const rmBadge = (window._canSeeRmStatus && row.rm_hold_reason) ? ` <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:10px;margin-left:4px;${row.rm_hold_reason === 'testing' ? 'background:#eff6ff;color:#1d4ed8;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;' : 'background:#fff7ed;color:#c2410c;border:1px solid #fed7aa;'}">${row.rm_hold_reason === 'testing' ? '<i class=\"fa fa-flask\"></i> Testing' : '<i class=\"fa fa-exclamation-triangle\"></i> Shortage'}</span>` : "";
1251:         v = `<span class="${cls}"${vendor}${clickAttr}>${v}</span>${rmBadge}`;
1252:       }
1253:       if (c.key === "final_status" && v) {
1254:         const cls = String(v).toLowerCase() === "completed" ? "badge-ok" : "badge-pending";
1255:         v = `<span class="${cls}">${v}</span>`;
1256:       }
1257:       const numericKeys = ["so_qty", "actual_qty", "total_days",
1258:         "remaining_days", "days_overdue", "wip_stage_days"];
1259:       const isNum = numericKeys.includes(c.key);
1260:       const tdStyle = isNum ? "text-align:center;min-width:36px;max-width:52px;" : "";
1261:       return `<td title="${String(formatCellValue(c.key, rawValue))}" style="${tdStyle}">${v}</td>`;
1262:     }).join("");

1264:       ? `<td style="text-align:center;min-width:58px;max-width:58px;">
1265:           <button type="button" title="Delete item" onclick="softDeletePpcItem(${index})" style="width:28px;height:28px;border:1px solid #fecaca;border-radius:4px;background:#fef2f2;color:#dc2626;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">
1266:             <i class="fa fa-trash" aria-hidden="true"></i>
1267:           </button>
1268:         </td>`
1269:       : "";

1271:     return `<tr class="${priorityClass}">${cells}${actionCell}</tr>`;
1272:   }).join("");

1194: 
1195: function getWipBadgeClass(row, value) {
1196:   if (isSubcontracted(row)) return "badge-subcontract";
1197:   const s = String(value || "").toLowerCase();
1198:   if (s === "store" || s === "complete" || s === "completed") return "badge-ok";
1199:   if (s === "pending") return "badge-pending";
1200:   return "badge-wip";
1201: }

1203: function sameProcess(a, b) {
1204:   return String(a || "").trim().toLowerCase() === String(b || "").trim().toLowerCase();
1205: }

1207: function getProcessVisualState(process, index, currentIndex, wipStatus) {
1208:   const wip = String(wipStatus || "").trim().toLowerCase();
1209:   if (wip === "store" || wip === "complete" || wip === "completed") return "completed";
1210:   if (currentIndex === -1) return process.is_completed ? "completed" : "pending";
1211:   if (index < currentIndex) return "completed";
1212:   if (index > currentIndex) return "pending";
1213:   return isSubcontracted(process) ? "subcontract" : "current";
1214: }

1216: // ── Render body ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1217: function renderBody(cols, rows) {
1218:   const tbody = document.getElementById("table-body");
1219:   const showDeleteAction = activeTab === "jc" && canDeletePpcItems();
1220:   if (!rows.length) {
1221:     tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${cols.length + (showDeleteAction ? 1 : 0)}"><div class="data-state">No records found</div></td></tr>`;
1222:     return;
1223:   }
1224:   tbody.innerHTML = rows.map((row, index) => {
1225:     const cells = cols.map(c => {
1226:       const rawValue = row[c.key];
1227:       let v = c.key === "wip_stage_days" ? (rawValue ?? 0) : formatCellValue(c.key, rawValue);
1228:       if (c.key === "job_card_no" && v) {
1229:         v = `<a class="jc-link" onclick="goToPage3('${String(v).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">${v}</a>`;
1230:         if (typeof canEditRowFields === "function" && canEditRowFields(row)) {
1231:           v += ` <i class="fa fa-pencil" style="margin-left:6px;color:var(--accent);cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;" title="Edit fields" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); openEditJobCardModalById('${String(row.job_card_no).replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', '${String(row.item_name).replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}')"></i>`;
1232:         }
1233:       }
1234:       if (c.key === "is_priority") {
1235:         const canEditPriority = hasPage5Field(row, "is_priority");
1236:         v = `<input type="checkbox" ${row.is_priority ? "checked" : ""} ${canEditPriority ? "" : "disabled"} onchange="togglePriorityImmediate(${Number(row.item_id) || 0}, '${row.job_card_no}', '${encodeURIComponent(row.item_name)}', this.checked, this)" style="width:16px;height:16px;cursor:${canEditPriority ? "pointer" : "not-allowed"};accent-color:#dc2626;" />`;
1237:       }
1238:       if (c.key === "quality_result") {
1239:         v = v === "OK" ? `<span class="badge-ok">OK</span>`
1240:           : v === "NOT OK" ? `<span class="badge-notok">Not OK</span>` : v;
1241:       }
1242:       if (c.key === "days_overdue" && v > 0) {
1243:         v = `<span class="badge-overdue">${v}d overdue</span>`;
1244:       }
1245:       if (c.key === "wip_status" && v) {
1246:         const cls = getWipBadgeClass(row, v);
1247:         const vendor = isSubcontracted(row) && row.vendor_name ? ` title="Subcontracting: ${row.vendor_name}"` : "";
1248:         const isStoreOrPending = ["store", "pending"].includes(String(rawValue || "").trim().toLowerCase());
1249:         const clickAttr = (isStoreOrPending || !canEditRowProcess(row)) ? "" : ` style="cursor:pointer;" onclick='openSharedStageModal(${JSON.stringify(row.job_card_no)}, ${JSON.stringify(row.item_name)})'`;
1250:         const rmBadge = (window._canSeeRmStatus && row.rm_hold_reason) ? ` <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:10px;margin-left:4px;${row.rm_hold_reason === 'testing' ? 'background:#eff6ff;color:#1d4ed8;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;' : 'background:#fff7ed;color:#c2410c;border:1px solid #fed7aa;'}">${row.rm_hold_reason === 'testing' ? '<i class=\"fa fa-flask\"></i> Testing' : '<i class=\"fa fa-exclamation-triangle\"></i> Shortage'}</span>` : "";
1251:         v = `<span class="${cls}"${vendor}${clickAttr}>${v}</span>${rmBadge}`;
1252:       }
1253:       if (c.key === "final_status" && v) {
1254:         const cls = String(v).toLowerCase() === "completed" ? "badge-ok" : "badge-pending";
1255:         v = `<span class="${cls}">${v}</span>`;
1256:       }
1257:       const numericKeys = ["so_qty", "actual_qty", "total_days",
1258:         "remaining_days", "days_overdue", "wip_stage_days"];
1259:       const isNum = numericKeys.includes(c.key);
1260:       const tdStyle = isNum ? "text-align:center;min-width:36px;max-width:52px;" : "";
1261:       return `<td title="${String(formatCellValue(c.key, rawValue))}" style="${tdStyle}">${v}</td>`;
1262:     }).join("");
1263:     const actionCell = showDeleteAction
1264:       ? `<td style="text-align:center;min-width:58px;max-width:58px;">
1265:           <button type="button" title="Delete item" onclick="softDeletePpcItem(${index})" style="width:28px;height:28px;border:1px solid #fecaca;border-radius:4px;background:#fef2f2;color:#dc2626;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">
1266:             <i class="fa fa-trash" aria-hidden="true"></i>
1267:           </button>
1268:         </td>`
1269:       : "";
1270:     const priorityClass = window._canSeePriorityColumn !== false && row.is_priority ? "row-priority" : "";

1275: // ── Priority toggle ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1276: // OPTION A — Immediate save, no confirmation (currently active)
1277: async function togglePriorityImmediate(itemId, jobCardNo, encodedItemName, isChecked, checkboxEl) {
1278:   const itemName = decodeURIComponent(encodedItemName);
1279:   try {
1280:     const res = await fetch("/api/job_card_item/priority", {
1281:       method: "POST",
1282:       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
1283:       body: JSON.stringify({ item_id: itemId || null, job_card_no: jobCardNo, item_name: itemName, is_priority: isChecked ? 1 : 0 })
1284:     });
1285:     const data = await res.json();
1286:     if (data.success) {
1287:       showToast(isChecked ? "Marked as Urgent" : "Marked as Regular", "success");
1288:       loadData();
1289:     } else {
1290:       showToast(data.error || "Failed to update priority", "error");
1291:       if (checkboxEl) checkboxEl.checked = !isChecked;
1292:     }
1293:   } catch (e) {
1294:     showToast("Error: " + (e.message || e), "error");
1295:     if (checkboxEl) checkboxEl.checked = !isChecked;
1296:   }
1297: }

1300: 
1301: function softDeletePpcItem(rowIndex) {
1302:   if (!canDeletePpcItems()) {
1303:     showToast("You do not have permission to delete job cards.", "error");
1304:     return;
1305:   }
1306: 
1307:   const row = (allData || [])[rowIndex];
1308: 
1309:   if (!row || !row.job_card_no) {
1310:     showToast("Could not find the Job Card number.", "error");
1311:     return;
1312:   }
1313: 
1314:   pendingDeleteRow = row;
1315:   openPermanentDeleteModal(row);
1316: }

1294:     showToast("Error: " + (e.message || e), "error");
1295:     if (checkboxEl) checkboxEl.checked = !isChecked;
1296:   }
1297: }

1299: let pendingDeleteRow = null;

1301: function softDeletePpcItem(rowIndex) {
1302:   if (!canDeletePpcItems()) {
1303:     showToast("You do not have permission to delete job cards.", "error");
1304:     return;
1305:   }
1306: 
1307:   const row = (allData || [])[rowIndex];
1308: 
1309:   if (!row || !row.job_card_no) {
1310:     showToast("Could not find the Job Card number.", "error");
1311:     return;
1312:   }
1313: 
1314:   pendingDeleteRow = row;
1315:   openPermanentDeleteModal(row);
1316: }

1581: function goPage(p) {
1582:   const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / perPage);
1583:   if (p < 1 || p > totalPages) return;
1584:   currentPage = p;
1585:   loadData();
1586: }
1587: 
1588: function renderRowCount() {
1589:   const start = (currentPage - 1) * perPage + 1;
1590:   const end = Math.min(currentPage * perPage, totalRows);
1591:   document.getElementById("row-count").textContent =
1592:     totalRows ? `Showing ${start}–${end} of ${totalRows} records` : "No records";
1593: }

1598:   const end = Math.min(currentPage * perPage, totalRows);
1599:   const supervisorName = activeTab === "jc" ? getSelectedSupervisorName() : "";
1600:   const supervisorText = supervisorName ? ` for ${supervisorName}` : "";
1601:   document.getElementById("row-count").textContent =
1602:     totalRows ? `Showing ${start}-${end} of ${totalRows} records${supervisorText}` : `No records${supervisorText}`;
1603: }

1777: async function exportExcel() {
1778:   showToast("Preparing export...", "success");
1779:   try {
1780:     const cfg = TAB[activeTab];
1781:     if (!cfg) return;
1782: 
1783:     // Build params same as current view but with no pagination
1784:     const search = document.getElementById("global-search").value.trim();
1785:     const p = new URLSearchParams({
1786:       search,
1787:       page: 1,
1788:       per_page: 99999,
1789:       sort: sortCol,
1790:       order: sortOrder
1791:     });
1792: 
1793:     // Apply same filters as current view
1794:     if (activeTab !== "jc") {
1795:       document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content select").forEach(sel => {
1796:         if (sel.value) p.set(sel.dataset.param, sel.value);
1797:       });
1798:       document.querySelectorAll("#filter-panel-content input[type='date']").forEach(inp => {
1799:         if (inp.value) p.set(inp.dataset.param, inp.value);
1800:       });
1801:     }
1802:     const filterDate = localStorage.getItem("jms_filter_date");
1803:     if (filterDate) p.set("filter_date", filterDate);
1804:     if (activeTab === "jc") {
1805:       Object.entries(excelFilters || {}).forEach(([columnKey, selectedValues]) => {
1806:         if (!Array.isArray(selectedValues) || selectedValues.length === 0) return;
1807:         selectedValues.forEach(v => p.append(`xf_${columnKey}`, v));
1808:       });
1809:       if (lastAuditTimeFilter.from) p.set("audit_time_from", lastAuditTimeFilter.from);
1810:       if (lastAuditTimeFilter.to) p.set("audit_time_to", lastAuditTimeFilter.to);
1811:       const supervisorId = getSelectedSupervisorId();
1812:       if (supervisorId) p.set("supervisor_user_id", supervisorId);
1813:     }
1814: 
1815:     const res = await fetch(`${cfg.api}?${p.toString()}`);
1816:     const data = await res.json();
1817:     if (!data.success) { showToast("Export failed: " + data.error, "error"); return; }
1818: 
1819:     const cols = getVisibleColumns(cfg.cols || []);
1820:     const headers = cols.map(c => c.label);
1821:     const rows = (data.data || []).map(r => cols.map(c => formatCellValue(c.key, r[c.key])));
1822: 
1823:     const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([headers, ...rows]);
1824:     const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
1825:     XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Data");
1826:     XLSX.writeFile(wb, "export.xlsx");
1827:     showToast(`Exported ${rows.length} records.`, "success");
1828:   } catch (e) {
1829:     showToast("Export failed: " + e.message, "error");
1830:   }
1831: }

1924: 
1925: function renderPSTable(rows) {
1926:   const COLS = [
1927:     { key: "job_card_no", label: "JC No" },
1928:     { key: "assembly_name", label: "Assembly" },
1929:     { key: "item_name", label: "Item Name" },
1930:     { key: "wip_status", label: "Current Stage" },
1931:     { key: "live_stage_days", label: "Days in Stage" },
1932:     { key: "pend_days", label: "Pend Days" },
1933:     { key: "next_process", label: "Next Stage" },
1934:     { key: "delivery_date", label: "Delivery Date" },
1935:   ];
1936: 
1937:   // Header
1938:   document.getElementById("ps-head").innerHTML =
1939:     `<tr>${COLS.map(c => `<th>${c.label}</th>`).join("")}</tr>`;
1940: 
1941:   const tbody = document.getElementById("ps-body");
1942:   if (!rows.length) {
1943:     tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${COLS.length}"><div class="data-state">No records found</div></td></tr>`;
1944:     return;
1945:   }
1946: 
1947:   tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(row => {
1948:     const cells = COLS.map(c => {
1949:       let v = row[c.key] ?? "";
1950:       if (DATE_KEYS.has(c.key)) v = formatDateForDisplay(v) || "";
1951: 
1952:       // WIP status badge
1953:       if (c.key === "wip_status" && v) {
1954:         const cls = getWipBadgeClass(row, v);
1955:         const vendor = isSubcontracted(row) && row.vendor_name ? ` title="Subcontracting: ${row.vendor_name}"` : "";
1956:         v = `<span class="${cls}"${vendor}>${v}</span>`;
1957:       }
1958: 
1959:       // Days in stage — colored by urgency
1960:       if (c.key === "live_stage_days") {
1961:         const days = parseInt(v) || 0;
1962:         const color = days > 10 ? "#dc2626" : days > 5 ? "#d97706" : "#16a34a";
1963:         const bg = days > 10 ? "#fef2f2" : days > 5 ? "#fffbeb" : "#f0fdf4";
1964:         const border = days > 10 ? "#fca5a5" : days > 5 ? "#fde68a" : "#86efac";

1900:     if (_psFilterDate) psParams.append("filter_date", _psFilterDate);
1901:     if (_psDelivFrom) psParams.append("delivery_from", _psDelivFrom);
1902:     if (_psDelivTo) psParams.append("delivery_to", _psDelivTo);
1903:     if (_psOverdue) psParams.append("overdue", _psOverdue);
1904:     if (_psUrgent) psParams.append("urgent_only", _psUrgent);
1905:     const res = await fetch(`/api/data/planning_sheet?${psParams.toString()}`);
1906:     const data = await res.json();
1907:     if (!data.success) { showToast(data.error, "error"); return; }
1908: 
1909:     psData = data.data;
1910:     renderPSTable(data.data);
1911: 
1912:     // Populate WIP filter
1913:     const sel = document.getElementById("ps-wip-filter");
1914:     if (sel.options.length <= 1 && data.wip_options?.length) {
1915:       data.wip_options.forEach(w => {
1916:         const opt = document.createElement("option");
1917:         opt.value = w; opt.textContent = w;
1918:         sel.appendChild(opt);
1919:       });
1920:     }
1921:     document.getElementById("ps-row-count").textContent = `${data.data.length} record(s)`;
1922:   } catch (e) { showToast("Error: " + (e.message || e), "error"); }
1923: }

1925: function renderPSTable(rows) {
1926:   const COLS = [
1927:     { key: "job_card_no", label: "JC No" },
1928:     { key: "assembly_name", label: "Assembly" },
1929:     { key: "item_name", label: "Item Name" },
1930:     { key: "wip_status", label: "Current Stage" },
1931:     { key: "live_stage_days", label: "Days in Stage" },
1932:     { key: "pend_days", label: "Pend Days" },
1933:     { key: "next_process", label: "Next Stage" },
1934:     { key: "delivery_date", label: "Delivery Date" },
1935:   ];
1936: 
1937:   // Header
1938:   document.getElementById("ps-head").innerHTML =
1939:     `<tr>${COLS.map(c => `<th>${c.label}</th>`).join("")}</tr>`;
1940: 
1941:   const tbody = document.getElementById("ps-body");
1942:   if (!rows.length) {
1943:     tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${COLS.length}"><div class="data-state">No records found</div></td></tr>`;
1944:     return;
1945:   }
1946: 
1947:   tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(row => {
1948:     const cells = COLS.map(c => {
1949:       let v = row[c.key] ?? "";
1950:       if (DATE_KEYS.has(c.key)) v = formatDateForDisplay(v) || "";
1951: 
1952:       // WIP status badge
1953:       if (c.key === "wip_status" && v) {
1954:         const cls = getWipBadgeClass(row, v);
1955:         const vendor = isSubcontracted(row) && row.vendor_name ? ` title="Subcontracting: ${row.vendor_name}"` : "";
1956:         v = `<span class="${cls}"${vendor}>${v}</span>`;
1957:       }
1958: 
1959:       // Days in stage — colored by urgency
1960:       if (c.key === "live_stage_days") {
1961:         const days = parseInt(v) || 0;
1962:         const color = days > 10 ? "#dc2626" : days > 5 ? "#d97706" : "#16a34a";
1963:         const bg = days > 10 ? "#fef2f2" : days > 5 ? "#fffbeb" : "#f0fdf4";
1964:         const border = days > 10 ? "#fca5a5" : days > 5 ? "#fde68a" : "#86efac";
1965:         v = `<span style="font-weight:700;color:${color};background:${bg};border:1px solid ${border};padding:2px 10px;border-radius:20px;">${days}d</span>`;
1966:       }
1967: 
1968:       // Next stage badge
1969:       if (c.key === "next_process") {
1970:         if (!v || v === "") v = `<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">—</span>`;
1971:         else v = `<span style="background:#eff6ff;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;color:#1a56db;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:20px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;">${v}</span>`;
1972:       }
1973: 
1974:       // Pend days — colored
1975:       if (c.key === "pend_days") {
1976:         if (v === null || v === "") { v = "—"; }
1977:         else {
1978:           const days = parseInt(v);
1979:           const color = days < 0 ? "#dc2626" : days <= 7 ? "#d97706" : "#16a34a";

2036:   ].filter(p => psData.some(r => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(r, p)));
2037: 
2038:   const endHeaders = [
2039:     "Total Days", "Pend Days", "Final Delivery Date", "Final Status"
2040:   ];
2041: 
2042:   const endKeys = [
2043:     "total_days", "pend_days", "delivery_date", "final_status"
2044:   ];
2045: 
2046:   const headers = [...fixedHeaders, ...processHeaders, ...endHeaders];
2047: 
2048:   const rows = psData.map(r => {
2049:     const fixedVals = fixedKeys.map(k => formatCellValue(k, r[k]));
2050:     const processVals = processHeaders.map(p => r[p] ?? 0);
2051:     const endVals = endKeys.map(k => formatCellValue(k, r[k]));
2052:     return [...fixedVals, ...processVals, ...endVals];
2053:   });
2054: 
2055:   const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([headers, ...rows]);
2056:   const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
2057: 
2058:   XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Planning Sheet");
2059:   XLSX.writeFile(wb, "planning_sheet.xlsx");
2060: }

2124: async function openSharedStageModal(jcNo, itemName) {
2125:   try {
2126:     const res = await fetch(`/api/quality_check/fetch/${encodeURIComponent(jcNo)}`);
2127:     const data = await res.json();
2128:     if (!data.success) { showToast(data.error || "Could not load job card", "error"); return; }
2129: 
2130:     sharedModalData = data;
2131:     const item = (data.items || []).find(it => it.item_name === itemName);
2132:     if (!item) { showToast("Item not found on this job card", "error"); return; }
2133: 
2134:     // Supervisor process-access pre-check (same rule as Page 3)
2135:     if (!isGaurangSpecialUser() && Array.isArray(data.my_accessible_processes)) {
2136:       const currentWip = (item.wip_status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
2137:       const hasAccess = data.my_accessible_processes.some(p => p.trim().toLowerCase() === currentWip);

2276:       });
2277:     } else {
2278:       res = await fetch("/api/wip/update", {
2279:         method: "POST",
2280:         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
2281:         body: JSON.stringify({
2282:           job_card_no: jcNo,
2283:           item_name: itemName,
2284:           new_stage: newStage,
2285:           changed_by: supervisor,
2286:           actual_qty: actualQty,
2287:           rejected_qty: 0,
2288:           rework_qty: 0,
2289:           rework_remarks: "",
2290:           stage_remark: stageRemark,
2291:         }),
2292:       });
2293:     }
2294: 
2295:     data = await res.json();
2296: 
2297:     if (data.success) {
2298:       closeSharedStageModal();
2299:       showToast(data.message || "Stage updated", "success");
2300: 
2301:       if (activeTab === "pr") {
2302:         loadProcessReport();
2303:       } else {
2304:         loadData();
2305:       }
2306:     } else {

2313: function validateSharedStageActualQty() {
2314:   const plannedQty = parseSharedStageQty(document.getElementById("modal-planned-qty")?.value);
2315:   const actualRaw = document.getElementById("modal-actual-qty")?.value;
2316:   if (actualRaw === "" || actualRaw == null) return true;
2317: 
2318:   const actualQty = parseSharedStageQty(actualRaw);
2319:   if (actualQty == null) {
2320:     showToast("Please enter a valid Actual Qty", "error");
2321:     document.getElementById("modal-actual-qty")?.focus();
2322:     return false;
2323:   }
2324: 
2325:   if (actualQty < 0) {
2326:     showToast("Actual Qty cannot be negative", "error");
2327:     document.getElementById("modal-actual-qty")?.focus();
2328:     return false;
2329:   }
2330: 
2331:   if (plannedQty != null && actualQty > plannedQty) {
2332:     showToast("Actual Qty cannot be more than Planned Qty", "error");
2333:     document.getElementById("modal-actual-qty")?.focus();
2334:     return false;
2335:   }
2336:   return true;
2337: }

2339: function parseSharedStageQty(value) {
2340:   if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return null;
2341:   const qty = Number(String(value).replace(/,/g, "").trim());
2342:   return Number.isFinite(qty) ? qty : null;
2343: }

2348:

2413:   if (!el) return;
2414:   el.disabled = !enabled;
2415:   el.style.background = enabled ? "" : "#f8fafc";

2417:   el.style.cursor = enabled ? "" : "not-allowed";

2442:   openEditJobCardModal(row);

2445: function openEditJobCardModal(row) {
2446:   if (!isEditableUser()) return;
2447: 
2448:   // Dispatch user — show simplified modal with only SO No, Customer Name, Delivery Date
2449:   if (isDispatchUser()) {
2450:     document.getElementById("ejc-job-card-no").value = row.job_card_no || "";
2451:     document.getElementById("ejc-so-no").value = row.so_no || "";
2452:     document.getElementById("ejc-customer-name").value = row.customer_name || "";
2453: 
2454:     // Hide all fields
2455:     ["ejc-job-card-no", "ejc-so-no", "ejc-customer-name", "ejc-parent-code",
2456:       "ejc-child-code", "ejc-work-order-no", "ejc-item-name", "ejc-material", "ejc-so-qty", "ejc-actual-qty",
2457:       "ejc-remarks", "ejc-is-subcontract", "ejc-vendor-name"].forEach(id => {
2458:         const wrap = document.getElementById(id)?.closest("div");
2459:         if (wrap) wrap.style.display = "none";
2460:       });
2461: 
2462:     // Show SO No and Customer Name as read-only
2463:     ["ejc-so-no", "ejc-customer-name"].forEach(id => {
2464:       const el = document.getElementById(id);
2465:       if (el) {
2466:         el.readOnly = true;
2467:         el.style.background = "#f8fafc";
2468:         el.closest("div").style.display = "";
2469:       }
2470:     });
2471: 
2472:     // Show delivery date
2473:     const deliveryWrap = document.getElementById("ejc-delivery-date-wrap");
2474:     if (deliveryWrap) {
2475:       deliveryWrap.style.display = "";
2476:       document.getElementById("ejc-delivery-date").value = row.delivery_date || "";
2477:     }
2478: 
2479:     editRowData = row;
2480:     document.getElementById("edit-jobcard-modal").classList.add("open");
2481:     return;
2482:   }
2483: 
2484:   if (!canEditRowFields(row)) {
2485:     showToast("You do not have rights to update this process.", "error");
2486:     return;
2487:   }
2488: 
2489:   editRowData = row;
2490: 
2491:   applyEditFieldPermissions();
2492: 
2493:   const setValue = (id, value) => {

2421:   const row = editRowData || {};
2422:   const canEditVendor = hasPage5Field(row, "vendor_name") || hasPage5Field(row, "subcontractor_name");
2423:   setEditInputState("ejc-job-card-no", hasPage5Field(row, "job_card_no"));
2424:   setEditInputState("ejc-so-no", hasPage5Field(row, "so_no"));
2425:   setEditInputState("ejc-customer-name", hasPage5Field(row, "customer_name"));
2426:   setEditInputState("ejc-parent-code", hasPage5Field(row, "parent_code"));
2427:   setEditInputState("ejc-child-code", hasPage5Field(row, "child_code"));
2428:   setEditInputState("ejc-work-order-no", hasPage5Field(row, "work_order_no"));
2429:   setEditInputState("ejc-item-name", hasPage5Field(row, "item_name"));
2430:   setEditInputState("ejc-material", hasPage5Field(row, "material"));
2431:   setEditInputState("ejc-so-qty", hasPage5Field(row, "so_qty"));
2432:   setEditInputState("ejc-actual-qty", hasPage5Field(row, "actual_qty"));
2433:   setEditInputState("ejc-remarks", hasPage5Field(row, "remarks"));

2496:   };
2497: 
2498:   setValue("ejc-job-card-no", row.job_card_no || "");
2499:   setValue("ejc-so-no", row.so_no || "");
2500:   setValue("ejc-customer-name", row.customer_name || "");

2865:     return;
2866:   }
2867:   const filtered = arAllRows.filter(row => {
2868:     return (
2869:       (row.assembly_name || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2870:       (row.parent_code || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2871:       (row.so_no || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2872:       (row.customer_name || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2873:       (row.children || []).some(c =>
2874:         (c.item_name || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2875:         (c.child_code || "").toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
2876:         (c.wip_status || "").toLowerCase().includes(search)
2877:       )
2878:     );
2879:   });
2880:   renderAssemblyReadiness(filtered);
2881: }

2890: // ── WIP Summary ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
2891: async function loadWipSummary() {
2892:   const fromDate = document.getElementById("ws-from-date").value;
2893:   const toDate = document.getElementById("ws-to-date").value;
2894:   const loading = document.getElementById("ws-loading");
2895:   const table = document.getElementById("ws-table");
2896:   const thead = document.getElementById("ws-thead");
2897:   const tbody = document.getElementById("ws-tbody");
2898: 
2899:   loading.textContent = "Loading...";
2900:   loading.style.display = "block";
2901:   table.style.display = "none";
2902: 
2903:   const params = new URLSearchParams();
2904:   if (fromDate) params.set("from_date", fromDate);
2905:   if (toDate) params.set("to_date", toDate);
2906: 
2907:   try {
2908:     const res = await fetch(`/api/wip_summary?${params.toString()}`);
2909:     const data = await res.json();
2910: 
2911:     if (!data.success || !data.rows.length) {
2912:       loading.textContent = "No data found for the selected date range.";
2913:       return;
2914:     }
2915: 
2916:     const processes = data.processes;
2917: 
2918:     // Build header
2919:     thead.innerHTML = `<tr>
2920:       <th style="padding:10px 14px; text-align:left; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.5px; white-space:nowrap; border-right:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);">Date</th>
2921:       ${processes.map(p => `<th style="padding:10px 14px; text-align:center; font-size:12px; letter-spacing:0.5px; white-space:nowrap; border-right:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);">${escapeHtml(p)}</th>`).join("")}
2922:     </tr>`;
2923: 
2924:     // Build body
2925:     tbody.innerHTML = data.rows.map((row, idx) => `
2926:       <tr style="background:${idx % 2 === 0 ? '#ffffff' : '#f1f5f9'};">
2927:         <td style="padding:9px 14px; font-weight:600; white-space:nowrap; border-right:1px solid var(--border); color:var(--text);">${row.date}</td>
2928:         ${processes.map(p => {
2929:       const val = row[p] || 0;
2930:       return `<td style="padding:9px 14px; text-align:center; border-right:1px solid var(--border); color:${val > 0 ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--muted)'}; font-weight:${val > 0 ? '700' : '400'}; ${val > 0 ? 'cursor:pointer;' : ''}" ${val > 0 ? `onclick="openWipDetailModal('${row.date}', '${p.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')"` : ''}>${val > 0 ? val : '—'}</td>`;
2931:     }).join("")}
2932:       </tr>
2933:     `).join("");
2934: 
2935:     loading.style.display = "none";
2936:     table.style.display = "table";
2937: 
2938:   } catch (e) {
2939:     loading.textContent = "Failed to load WIP Summary. Please try again.";
2940:   }
2941: }

2966:         </div>

2974:       </div>
2975:     </div>
2976:   `;

3072: async function confirmDdReasonAndSave() {
3073:   const reason = (document.getElementById("dd-reason-input").value || "").trim();
3074:   if (!reason) {
3075:     document.getElementById("dd-reason-error").style.display = "block";
3076:     return;
3077:   }
3078:   document.getElementById("dd-reason-error").style.display = "none";
3079:   document.getElementById("dd-reason-modal").style.display = "none";
3080: 
3081:   const payload = { ...window._pendingDdPayload, dd_change_reason: reason };
3082:   window._pendingDdPayload = null;
3083: 
3084:   try {
3085:     const res = await fetch("/api/job_card/update_fields", {
3086:       method: "POST",
3087:       headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
3088:       body: JSON.stringify(payload)
3089:     });
3090:     const data = await res.json();
3091:     if (data.success) {
3092:       showToast(data.message || "Delivery date updated.", "success");
3093:       closeEditJobCardModal();
3094:       loadData();
3095:     } else {
3096:       showToast(data.error || "Update failed", "error");
3097:     }
3098:   } catch (e) {
3099:     showToast("Error: " + (e.message || e), "error");
3100:   }
3101: }
